        Idea:
light cleanup of six bot bugs — see notes

User notes and answers:
- ANSWERS TO PLAN QUESTIONS — full bug list:

(1) DM context bug — src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:246-275
Only the guild branch attaches replied_to/recent_messages; the DM branch is missing both. Fix: mirror the guild branch's reply/history fetch into the DM branch (DMChannel supports .history() the same way), and surface the replied_to anchor more loudly to the model so it knows which prior message the user is pointing at.

(2) Crash on follow-up tweet — admin_chat_cog.py:333
discord.errors.DiscordServerError (503 from Discord's edge) raised inside message.channel.send bubbled to the cog catch-all at lines 348-350 which then echoed the raw exception text into the chat. Fix: small retry (1-2 retries with short backoff) on discord.HTTPException 5xx around the per-part channel.send, and stop pasting raw exception strings — log with exc_info, send neutral user copy.

(3) Zapier IG/TikTok/YouTube webhooks return 404 — they're dead.
User has approved REMOVING these destinations entirely from src/features/sharing/subfeatures/social_poster.py and any callsites/tools that reference them. Twitter stays. Be thorough about removing references — don't leave dead code.

(4) share_to_social blows up on ForumChannel — src/features/admin_chat/tools.py:1022
ForumChannel has no fetch_message — needs to resolve to the thread first (ForumChannel posts ARE threads — get the thread via guild.get_thread / channel.get_thread / bot.fetch_channel before calling fetch_message).

(5) query_table — src/features/admin_chat/tools.py:1536
Referencing columns that don't exist (discord_messages.author_name, shared_posts.created_at). Verify the actual schema and either fix the tool description / column-mapping logic or update what the agent is told about column names.

(6) src/common/storage_handler.py:143
'deque mutated during iteration' race when serialising the message batch to Supabase — fix by taking a snapshot/copy of the deque before iterating/serialising.

ANSWERS TO META QUESTIONS:
- One bundled commit is fine (do not need six separate commits).
- Do not skip any bugs.
- Leave the unrelated working-tree changes (summariser.py, scripts/) alone.
- Notes are now provided here; no separate file.

        Idea:
light cleanup of six bot bugs — see notes

User notes and answers:
- ANSWERS TO PLAN QUESTIONS — full bug list:

(1) DM context bug — src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:246-275
Only the guild branch attaches replied_to/recent_messages; the DM branch is missing both. Fix: mirror the guild branch's reply/history fetch into the DM branch (DMChannel supports .history() the same way), and surface the replied_to anchor more loudly to the model so it knows which prior message the user is pointing at.

(2) Crash on follow-up tweet — admin_chat_cog.py:333
discord.errors.DiscordServerError (503 from Discord's edge) raised inside message.channel.send bubbled to the cog catch-all at lines 348-350 which then echoed the raw exception text into the chat. Fix: small retry (1-2 retries with short backoff) on discord.HTTPException 5xx around the per-part channel.send, and stop pasting raw exception strings — log with exc_info, send neutral user copy.

(3) Zapier IG/TikTok/YouTube webhooks return 404 — they're dead.
User has approved REMOVING these destinations entirely from src/features/sharing/subfeatures/social_poster.py and any callsites/tools that reference them. Twitter stays. Be thorough about removing references — don't leave dead code.

(4) share_to_social blows up on ForumChannel — src/features/admin_chat/tools.py:1022
ForumChannel has no fetch_message — needs to resolve to the thread first (ForumChannel posts ARE threads — get the thread via guild.get_thread / channel.get_thread / bot.fetch_channel before calling fetch_message).

(5) query_table — src/features/admin_chat/tools.py:1536
Referencing columns that don't exist (discord_messages.author_name, shared_posts.created_at). Verify the actual schema and either fix the tool description / column-mapping logic or update what the agent is told about column names.

(6) src/common/storage_handler.py:143
'deque mutated during iteration' race when serialising the message batch to Supabase — fix by taking a snapshot/copy of the deque before iterating/serialising.

ANSWERS TO META QUESTIONS:
- One bundled commit is fine (do not need six separate commits).
- Do not skip any bugs.
- Leave the unrelated working-tree changes (summariser.py, scripts/) alone.
- Notes are now provided here; no separate file.

  "meta_commentary": "Six localized Discord-bot bug fixes landing as one bundled commit. Files and line numbers in the plan were verified by the planner against the actual code, so trust them as starting anchors but re-confirm before each edit (line numbers may have drifted by small amounts). Execution order is deliberate: T1+T2 share a file (open it once), T3 is a one-line easy win, T4+T5+T6 are the contiguous Zapier-removal block (do them together so the diff is coherent), T7 and T8 are isolated, and T9 validates and commits. The two CRITICAL success criteria \\u2014 that Bugs 1 and 2 are FULLY solved by the proposed changes \\u2014 are enforced by SC1, SC2, and the static re-read in T9(d); do not skip those.   "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/brain-of-bndc/src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py\n@@\n             # In public channels, reply to the original message for the first response\n             reply_ref = message if not is_dm else None\n+\n+            async def _send_with_retry(channel, content_to_send: str, reference=None):\n+                backoffs = (0.5, 1.5)\n+                for attempt in range(len(backoffs) + 1):\n+                    try:\n+                        return await channel.send(content_to_send, reference=reference)\n+                    except discord.HTTPException as exc:\n+                        if exc.status < 500 or attempt == len(backoffs):\n+                            raise\n+                        await asyncio.sleep(backoffs[attempt])\n \n             for response in responses:\n                 # Skip empty responses\n                 if not response or not response.strip():\n                     continue\n@@\n                     total_chars += len(part)\n \n                     # Handle long messages by splitting\n                     if len(part) <= 2000:\n-                        await message.channel.send(part, reference=reply_ref)\n+                        await _send_with_retry(message.channel, part, reference=reply_ref)\n                         messages_sent += 1\n                     else:\n                         # Split into chunks\n                         chunks = [part[i:i+1990] for i in range(0, len(part), 1990)]\n                         for chunk in chunks:\n                             if chunk.strip():\n-                                await message.channel.send(chunk, reference=reply_ref)\n+                                await _send_with_retry(message.channel, chunk, reference=reply_ref)\n                                 messages_sent += 1\n@@\n-        except Exception as e:\n-            logger.error(f\"[AdminChat] Error processing message: {e}\", exc_info=True)\n-            await message.channel.send(f\"Sorry, I encountered an error: {str(e)}\")\n+        except Exception:\n+            logger.exception(\"[AdminChat] Error processing message\")\n+            try:\n+                await message.channel.send(\"Sorry, something went wrong on my side. Try again in a moment.\")\n+            except Exception:\n+                logger.exception(\"[AdminChat] Failed to send neutral error message\")\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
light cleanup of six bot bugs — see notes

User notes and answers:
- ANSWERS TO PLAN QUESTIONS — full bug list:

(1) DM context bug — src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:246-275
Only the guild branch attaches replied_to/recent_messages; the DM branch is missing both. Fix: mirror the guild branch's reply/history fetch into the DM branch (DMChannel supports .history() the same way), and surface the replied_to anchor more loudly to the model so it knows which prior message the user is pointing at.

(2) Crash on follow-up tweet — admin_chat_cog.py:333
discord.errors.DiscordServerError (503 from Discord's edge) raised inside message.channel.send bubbled to the cog catch-all at lines 348-350 which then echoed the raw exception text into the chat. Fix: small retry (1-2 retries with short backoff) on discord.HTTPException 5xx around the per-part channel.send, and stop pasting raw exception strings — log with exc_info, send neutral user copy.

(3) Zapier IG/TikTok/YouTube webhooks return 404 — they're dead.
User has approved REMOVING these destinations entirely from src/features/sharing/subfeatures/social_poster.py and any callsites/tools that reference them. Twitter stays. Be thorough about removing references — don't leave dead code.

(4) share_to_social blows up on ForumChannel — src/features/admin_chat/tools.py:1022
ForumChannel has no fetch_message — needs to resolve to the thread first (ForumChannel posts ARE threads — get the thread via guild.get_thread / channel.get_thread / bot.fetch_channel before calling fetch_message).

(5) query_table — src/features/admin_chat/tools.py:1536
Referencing columns that don't exist (discord_messages.author_name, shared_posts.created_at). Verify the actual schema and either fix the tool description / column-mapping logic or update what the agent is told about column names.

(6) src/common/storage_handler.py:143
'deque mutated during iteration' race when serialising the message batch to Supabase — fix by taking a snapshot/copy of the deque before iterating/serialising.

ANSWERS TO META QUESTIONS:
- One bundled commit is fine (do not need six separate commits).
- Do not skip any bugs.
- Leave the unrelated working-tree changes (summariser.py, scripts/) alone.
- Notes are now provided here; no separate file.

  "meta_commentary": "Six localized Discord-bot bug fixes landing as one bundled commit. Files and line numbers in the plan were verified by the planner against the actual code, so trust them as starting anchors but re-confirm before each edit (line numbers may have drifted by small amounts). Execution order is deliberate: T1+T2 share a file (open it once), T3 is a one-line easy win, T4+T5+T6 are the contiguous Zapier-removal block (do them together so the diff is coherent), T7 and T8 are isolated, and T9 validates and commits. The two CRITICAL success criteria \\u2014 that Bugs 1 and 2 are FULLY solved by the proposed changes \\u2014 are enforced by SC1, SC2, and the static re-read in T9(d); do not skip those. The user's #1 pain point is the catch-all that echoed raw exception text into chat, so be especially disciplined that NO str(e) makes it into user-facing copy in Bug 2. For Bug 3, the user said 'be thorough \\u2014 don\\u2019t leave dead code' \\u2014 the grep sweep in T6 is the contract, not optional. For Bug 1, the 'surface the anchor more loudly' requirement means an explicit key in channel_context, not just a hope that the model will notice replied_to.         Idea:
light cleanup of six bot bugs — see notes

User notes and answers:
- ANSWERS TO PLAN QUESTIONS — full bug list:

(1) DM context bug — src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:246-275
Only the guild branch attaches replied_to/recent_messages; the DM branch is missing both. Fix: mirror the guild branch's reply/history fetch into the DM branch (DMChannel supports .history() the same way), and surface the replied_to anchor more loudly to the model so it knows which prior message the user is pointing at.

(2) Crash on follow-up tweet — admin_chat_cog.py:333
discord.errors.DiscordServerError (503 from Discord's edge) raised inside message.channel.send bubbled to the cog catch-all at lines 348-350 which then echoed the raw exception text into the chat. Fix: small retry (1-2 retries with short backoff) on discord.HTTPException 5xx around the per-part channel.send, and stop pasting raw exception strings — log with exc_info, send neutral user copy.

(3) Zapier IG/TikTok/YouTube webhooks return 404 — they're dead.
User has approved REMOVING these destinations entirely from src/features/sharing/subfeatures/social_poster.py and any callsites/tools that reference them. Twitter stays. Be thorough about removing references — don't leave dead code.

(4) share_to_social blows up on ForumChannel — src/features/admin_chat/tools.py:1022
ForumChannel has no fetch_message — needs to resolve to the thread first (ForumChannel posts ARE threads — get the thread via guild.get_thread / channel.get_thread / bot.fetch_channel before calling fetch_message).

(5) query_table — src/features/admin_chat/tools.py:1536
Referencing columns that don't exist (discord_messages.author_name, shared_posts.created_at). Verify the actual schema and either fix the tool description / column-mapping logic or update what the agent is told about column names.

(6) src/common/storage_handler.py:143
'deque mutated during iteration' race when serialising the message batch to Supabase — fix by taking a snapshot/copy of the deque before iterating/serialising.

ANSWERS TO META QUESTIONS:
- One bundled commit is fine (do not need six separate commits).
- Do not skip any bugs.
- Leave the unrelated working-tree changes (summariser.py, scripts/) alone.
- Notes are now provided here; no separate file.

import StorageHandler\n+\n+\n+class FakeTable:\n+    def upsert(self, batch):\n+        self.batch = batch\n+        return self\n+\n+    def execute(self):\n+        return {\"ok\": True}\n+\n+\n+class FakeSupabase:\n+    def table(self, _name):\n+        return FakeTable()\n+\n+\n+class SlowMessage(dict):\n+    def __init__(self, *args, trigger_event: threading.Event, **kwargs):\n+        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)\n+        self._trigger_event = trigger_event\n+        self._triggered = False\n+\n+    def get(self, key, default=None):\n+        if not self._triggered:\n+            self._triggered = True\n+            self._trigger_event.set()\n+            time.sleep(0.05)\n+        return super().get(key, default)\n+\n+\n+async def main():\n+    handler = StorageHandler.__new__(StorageHandler)\n+    handler.supabase_client = FakeSupabase()\n+    handler.batch_size = 100\n+\n+    mutate_now = threading.Event()\n+    messages = deque(\n+        SlowMessage(\n+            {\n+                \"message_id\": str(i),\n+                \"channel_id\": \"10\",\n+                \"author_id\": \"20\",\n+                \"content\": f\"message {i}\",\n+                \"created_at\": \"2026-04-08T00:00:00\",\n+            },\n+            trigger_event=mutate_now,\n+        )\n+        for i in range(3)\n+    )\n+\n+    def mutate_deque():\n+        mutate_now.wait(timeout=1)\n+        messages.append(\n+            {\n+                \"message_id\": \"late\",\n+                \"channel_id\": \"10\",\n+                \"author_id\": \"20\",\n+                \"content\": \"late message\",\n+                \"created_at\": \"2026-04-08T00:00:00\",\n+            }\n+        )\n+\n+    worker = threading.Thread(target=mutate_deque, daemon=True)\n+    worker.start()\n+    stored = await handler.store_messages_to_supabase(messages)\n+    worker.join(timeout=1)\n+\n+    assert stored == 3, f\"expected 3 stored messages, got {stored}\"\n+    assert len(messages) == 4, f\"expected source deque mutation to occur, len={len(messages)}\"\n+    print(\"deque snapshot repro passed\")\n+\n+\n+if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n+    asyncio.run(main())\n*** End Patch\n"

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/brain-of-bndc

        Idea:
light cleanup of six bot bugs — see notes

User notes and answers:
- ANSWERS TO PLAN QUESTIONS — full bug list:

(1) DM context bug — src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:246-275
Only the guild branch attaches replied_to/recent_messages; the DM branch is missing both. Fix: mirror the guild branch's reply/history fetch into the DM branch (DMChannel supports .history() the same way), and surface the replied_to anchor more loudly to the model so it knows which prior message the user is pointing at.

(2) Crash on follow-up tweet — admin_chat_cog.py:333
discord.errors.DiscordServerError (503 from Discord's edge) raised inside message.channel.send bubbled to the cog catch-all at lines 348-350 which then echoed the raw exception text into the chat. Fix: small retry (1-2 retries with short backoff) on discord.HTTPException 5xx around the per-part channel.send, and stop pasting raw exception strings — log with exc_info, send neutral user copy.

(3) Zapier IG/TikTok/YouTube webhooks return 404 — they're dead.
User has approved REMOVING these destinations entirely from src/features/sharing/subfeatures/social_poster.py and any callsites/tools that reference them. Twitter stays. Be thorough about removing references — don't leave dead code.

(4) share_to_social blows up on ForumChannel — src/features/admin_chat/tools.py:1022
ForumChannel has no fetch_message — needs to resolve to the thread first (ForumChannel posts ARE threads — get the thread via guild.get_thread / channel.get_thread / bot.fetch_channel before calling fetch_message).

(5) query_table — src/features/admin_chat/tools.py:1536
Referencing columns that don't exist (discord_messages.author_name, shared_posts.created_at). Verify the actual schema and either fix the tool description / column-mapping logic or update what the agent is told about column names.

(6) src/common/storage_handler.py:143
'deque mutated during iteration' race when serialising the message batch to Supabase — fix by taking a snapshot/copy of the deque before iterating/serialising.

ANSWERS TO META QUESTIONS:
- One bundled commit is fine (do not need six separate commits).
- Do not skip any bugs.
- Leave the unrelated working-tree changes (summariser.py, scripts/) alone.
- Notes are now provided here; no separate file.
